local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")
local HttpService = game:GetService("HttpService")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")

local Config = {}
local ConfigPath = "StealAnEgg_config.json"
local function loadConfig()
	if isfile and isfile(ConfigPath) then
		local ok, data = pcall(function()
			return HttpService:JSONDecode(readfile(ConfigPath))
		end)
		if ok and type(data) == "table" then
			Config = data
		end
	end
end
local function saveConfig()
	if writefile then
		pcall(function()
			writefile(ConfigPath, HttpService:JSONEncode(Config))
		end)
	end
end
loadConfig()

local function getFlag(flag, default)
	if flag == nil or flag == "" then
		return default
	end
	if Config[flag] == nil then
		Config[flag] = default
	end
	return Config[flag]
end
local function setFlag(flag, value)
	if flag == nil or flag == "" then
		return
	end
	Config[flag] = value
	saveConfig()
end

pcall(function()
	local old = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("StealAnEggUI")
	if old then
		old:Destroy()
	end
end)

do
	local dragging, start, startPos
	titleBar.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			dragging = true
			start = input.Position
			startPos = main.Position
			input.Changed:Connect(function()
				if input.UserInputState == Enum.UserInputState.End then
					dragging = false
				end
			end)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			local d = input.Position - start
			main.Position = UDim2.new(startPos.X.Scale, startPos.X.Offset + d.X, startPos.Y.Scale, startPos.Y.Offset + d.Y)
		end
	end)
end

local function clearContent()
	for _, ch in ipairs(content:GetChildren()) do
		if not ch:IsA("UIListLayout") and not ch:IsA("UIPadding") then
			ch:Destroy()
		end
	end
end

local function makeToggle(name, flag)
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.Parent = content
	Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
	local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -60, 1, 0)
	lbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 0)
	lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	lbl.TextSize = 13
	lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(230, 230, 235)
	lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	lbl.Text = name
	lbl.Parent = row
	local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	btn.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(42, 22)
	btn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -50, 0.5, -11)
	btn.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	btn.TextSize = 11
	btn.Parent = row
	Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 11)
	local function paint(on)
		btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 40) or Color3.fromRGB(55, 55, 65)
		btn.Text = on and "ON" or "OFF"
		btn.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20) or Color3.fromRGB(180, 180, 190)
	end
	local on = getFlag(flag, false) == true
	paint(on)
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		on = not on
		setFlag(flag, on)
		paint(on)
	end)
end

	btn.Text = name
	btn.Parent = content
	Instance.new("UICorner", btn).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		print("[StealAnEgg]", name)
	end)
end

local function makeSlider(name, flag, minV, maxV)
	minV = tonumber(minV) or 0
	maxV = tonumber(maxV) or 100
	local row = Instance.new("Frame")
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 38)
	row.BorderSizePixel = 0
	row.Parent = content
	Instance.new("UICorner", row).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
	local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -12, 0, 18)
	lbl.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 4)
	lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	lbl.TextSize = 12
	lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 230)
	lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	local val = tonumber(getFlag(flag, minV)) or minV
	lbl.Text = string.format("%s: %s", name, tostring(val))
	lbl.Parent = row
	local bar = Instance.new("TextButton")
	bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 10)
	bar.Position = UDim2.fromOffset(10, 26)
	bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(50, 50, 60)
	bar.Text = ""
	bar.Parent = row
	Instance.new("UICorner", bar).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	local fill = Instance.new("Frame")
	fill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp((val - minV) / math.max(maxV - minV, 1), 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
	fill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 40)
	fill.BorderSizePixel = 0
	fill.Parent = bar
	Instance.new("UICorner", fill).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
	bar.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local steps = { minV, (minV + maxV) / 2, maxV }
		local best, bd = steps[1], math.huge
		for _, s in ipairs(steps) do
			local d = math.abs(s - val)
			if d < bd and s ~= val then
				best, bd = s, d
			end
		end
		val = best
		setFlag(flag, val)
		fill.Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp((val - minV) / math.max(maxV - minV, 1), 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)
		lbl.Text = string.format("%s: %s", name, tostring(val))
	end)
end

	lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	lbl.Font = Enum.Font.Gotham
	lbl.TextSize = 12
	lbl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 210)
	lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	lbl.Text = name .. "  (list)"
	lbl.Parent = row
end

local function showTab(tabName)
	clearContent()
	local page = PAGE_DATA[tabName]
	if not page then
		return
	end
	sectionLabel(tabName)
	for _, t in ipairs(page.toggles) do
		makeToggle(t.name, t.flag)
	end
	for _, d in ipairs(page.dropdowns) do
		makeDropdown(d.name, d.flag)
	end
	for _, s in ipairs(page.sliders) do
		makeSlider(s.name, s.flag, s.min, s.max)
	end
	for _, b in ipairs(page.buttons) do
		makeButton(b.name)
	end
end

local tabButtons = {}
local order = {}
for name in pairs(PAGE_DATA) do
	table.insert(order, name)
end
table.sort(order)

for _, tabName in ipairs(order) do
	local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
	b.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(72, 28)
	b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
	b.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBold
	b.TextSize = 11
	b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 230)
	b.Text = tabName
	b.Parent = tabBar
	Instance.new("UICorner", b).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 6)
	tabButtons[tabName] = b
	b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		for n, btn in pairs(tabButtons) do
			local on = n == tabName
			btn.BackgroundColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 40) or Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 50)
			btn.TextColor3 = on and Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20) or Color3.fromRGB(220, 220, 230)
		end
		showTab(tabName)
	end)
end

local first = order[1]
if first then
	tabButtons[first].BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 120, 40)
	tabButtons[first].TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 20, 20)
	showTab(first)
end

genv.StealAnEggConfig = Config
genv.StealAnEggUI = gui

-- Function [0]

-- Function [1]
Functions[1] = function(t, _t2, U, _U2, _U3)return function()local U,j=type(isfile)=="function"and type(readfile)=="function",type(writefile)=="function";
local function g()if rawget(_G,"BigFrootFreshLib")==true or not U then return nil;
end;
local U;
pcall(function()local H=isfile;
if H("BigFroot/blush_cache_at.txt")then U=tonumber(readfile("BigFroot/blush_cache_at.txt"));

if g>0 then j=t[4][6][t[4][3]](U);
if j==nil then return"weight unknown";
end;
if j>=g then return("%.0f kg >= limit"):format(j);
end;
end;
if H>0 then g,j=pcall(t[5].rate,U);
j=g and(tonumber(j))or nil;
if j==nil then return"rate unknown";
end;
if j>=H then return("%d/s >= limit"):format(math.floor(j));
end;
end;
return"sell";
end;
end

-- Function [11268]
Functions[11268] = function(t, _t2, U, _U2)return function()if t[1].Controls.FusePredict==nil then return;
end;
if not t[2]("fusePredict",2)then return;
end;
local U=t[1].fuseLoaded();
if#U==0 then t[3](t[1].Controls.FusePredict,"Nothing in the machine.");
return;
end;
local j,g={},0;
for H,y in ipairs(U)do local p=tonumber(y.item.Scale)or 0;
g+=p;
local r=y.item.Mutations;
y=type(r)=="table"and#r>0 and(table.concat(r,", "))or"Normal";
j[#j+1]=("Pet #%d: %.2fx | %s"):format(H,p,y);
end;

j[#j+1]=("Load %d more to fuse."):format(3-#U);
t[3](t[1].Controls.FusePredict,table.concat(j,"\10"));
return;
end;

y[#y+1]={tier=F,w=W};
p+=W;
end;

end;
end;
g=t[4][6][t[4][3]]();
local r,F,W,_=type(g)=="table"and g.Gamepasses or nil,U[1].item.Category;

end;
end;
end;
end;

j[#j+1]=("Income: $%s/s ~ $%s/s"):format(t[6][6][t[6][3]](W),t[6][6][t[6][3]](_));

end;

-- Function [12120]
Functions[12120] = function(t, _t2, U)return function(U, j, g, H)if U:GetAttribute("Phase")~="Ball"then return g;
end;
local y={};
local p=t[1].scrLitCoil(U);
if p~=nil then y[#y+1]={p=p.p,r=p.r+2};
end;
if H~="the ball"then p=U:FindFirstChild("Ball");
local r=p~=nil and(p:IsA("Model"))and p.PrimaryPart or nil;
if r~=nil then y[#y+1]={p=r.Position,r=t[1].scrBallRadius(U)+4};
end;
end;
U,H=j.X,j.Z;
local t,r=g.X-U,g.Z-H;
p=math.sqrt(t*t+r*r);
if p<0.1 then return g;
end;
t/=p;
r/=p;
local F,W=math.huge;
for _,c in y,nil,nil do local y,f,w=c.p.X,c.p.Z,c.r;
_,c=g.X-y,g.Z-f;
if _*_+c*c>=w*w then local _=math.clamp((y-U)*t+(f-H)*r,0,p);
local p,c=y-(U+t*_),f-(H+r*_);
if _<F and p*p+c*c<w*w then local U,H=-r;
if p*U+c*t>0 then U,H=-U,-t;
else H=t;
end;
F,W=_,(Vector3.new(y+U*(w+4),j.Y,f+H*(w+4)));
end;
end;
end;
return W or g;
end;
end

end;

y=function()return r and r.Parent and r.Position or nil;
end;

return;
end;
p="a crystal";
end;

return;
end;

if t[1].scrBossInArena()then if not U.running then U.running=true;
task.spawn(function()pcall(t[1].scrBossFight);
U.running=false;
t[1].Busy=false;
end);
end;
return;
end;
if not t[1].scrBossPortalUp()then U.portalSeen=false;
local j,g=t[4]:GetServerTimeNow(),U.seenStart or 0;
local H=g+math.ceil((j-g)/1800)*1800;
g=math.max(0,math.floor(H-j));
t[3](t[1].Controls.ScrBossStatus,("Next Dr. Scramble fight in %d:%02d%s."):format(math.floor(g/60),g%60,U.seenStart and""or" (estimated)"));
return;
end;
if not U.portalSeen then U.portalSeen=true;
U.seenStart=t[4]:GetServerTimeNow();
end;
if t[1].Busy then t[3](t[1].Controls.ScrBossStatus,"Portal is up -- finishing this steal, then going in.");
return;
end;
if os.clock()-U.enterAt<5 then return;
end;
if t[1].bossBat()==nil then t[3](t[1].Controls.ScrBossStatus,"No bat -- resetting to get it back.");
pcall(t[1].ensureBat);
return;
end;
U.enterAt=os.clock();
t[3](t[1].Controls.ScrBossStatus,"Portal is up -- going in.");
U.entering=true;
t[1].Busy=true;
pcall(t[1].scrBossEnter);
if not t[1].scrBossInArena()then U.entering=false;
t[1].Busy=false;
t[3](t[1].Controls.ScrBossStatus,"Could not get into the portal -- retrying.");
return;
end;
U.running=true;
task.spawn(function()pcall(t[1].scrBossFight);
U.running=false;
U.entering=false;
t[1].Busy=false;
end);
end;
end
